# Múi giờ cố định cho toàn hệ thống (utils + Core UI) - Approved Plan

> Snapshot of what the user approved at the `sdcorejs-plan` gate. Do not edit by hand; re-author through `sdcorejs-plan` if the contract changes.

## Approved contract

# Plan - Múi giờ cố định cho toàn hệ thống (utils + Core UI) - 2026-10-16-16-46

- Approved spec: `.sdcorejs/specs/angular/2026-10-16-15-37-core-ui-fixed-timezone-r3.md` (`sha256:v1:ee30a3ada737f889bda593f0f5674d63d25c37a2e23de1ad065b37907eede067`).
- Approved architecture: `.sdcorejs/architecture/angular/2026-10-16-15-55-core-ui-fixed-timezone.md` (`sha256:v1:4d194a5e8ce45efd229b247c3b0433a639f5035c044e8127e14b530a81b1d217`).

> **Revision 3** (thay cho `.sdcorejs/plans/angular/2026-10-16-16-46-core-ui-fixed-timezone-r2.md`). Lý do: khi làm TASK-003, guard tài liệu của site nằm ở `site/src/content/api.spec.ts`, `registry.spec.ts`, `render.spec.ts`, và docs của `FilterUtilities` nằm ở `site/src/content/api/function-entries-b.ts`; còn `site/src/content/api-data.ts` gom danh sách bằng spread nên không cần sửa. Chỉ danh sách file của TASK-003 đổi; task khác, bằng chứng và AC giữ nguyên.
>
> **Revision 2** (thay cho `.sdcorejs/plans/angular/2026-10-16-16-46-core-ui-fixed-timezone.md`). Lý do: kiểm tra `prepareExecution` trước khi sửa file cho thấy phạm vi ghi đã duyệt chỉ có đường dẫn của `sdcorejs-angular`; `README.md` nằm trong `prohibited_paths` nên chặn TASK-003 của utils; và `repository_plan` chưa có step theo từng repo. Bản này gộp đường dẫn của utils vào `allowed_paths`, bỏ `README.md` khỏi `prohibited_paths` (README của Core UI vẫn không nằm trong `allowed_paths`), và thêm `repository_plan` có step theo repo. Task, bằng chứng và AC giữ nguyên.

## Scope

Hai repo, làm theo thứ tự:

1. **`sdcorejs-ultis`:** thêm API múi giờ opt-in vào `@sdcorejs/utils` (bản minor 1.3.0), kèm docs và changeset. Người dùng merge rồi publish qua changesets.
2. **`sdcorejs-angular`:**
   - thêm `ISdCoreConfiguration.timeZone` và `SdDateTimeService`;
   - nối service vào pipe, ba form ngày, table, import-excel, form-generic, history, file-explorer;
   - cập nhật docs, Showcase, CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]`;
   - pin utils 1.3.0 rồi `npm run sync` sang v20/v21/v22.

Không tag, không publish Core UI.

## Execution context

- Track: `angular`. Profile: `core-ui-angular` (repo thư viện). Target root kind: `target-project`.
- Test: **TDD** ở mọi khu vực có code.
  - Mỗi cặp RED → GREEN dùng file `*.time-zone.spec.ts` mới, đặt cạnh spec cũ.
  - Spec cũ giữ nguyên làm bằng chứng AC-015 (chế độ local không đổi).
- Chạy song song: **không**. Utils phải xong trước vì là API mới, service phải có trước mọi khu vực. Phase 5–6 tách file nhưng dùng chung service và Karma.
- Node `22.22.3`; npm, vì cả hai repo đều có `package-lock.json`.
- Nhánh:
  - Core UI: `claude/core-ui-fixed-timezone`, từ `origin/main` `5f692cd34`.
  - Utils: worktree mới `C:/wt/utils-time-zone`, nhánh `feat/date-time-zone`, từ `9eb3052c`. Checkout chính của utils đang ở nhánh khác nên không đụng vào.

## Luồng release giữa hai repo (D-023)

1. **Phase 1 (utils):** TASK-001 → TASK-003, rồi mở PR vào `main` của `sdcorejs-ultis`.
2. **Điểm dừng, người dùng làm:**
   - merge PR utils;
   - changesets bot mở PR "version packages", merge PR này;
   - CI publish `@sdcorejs/utils@1.3.0`.
3. **Phase 2–7 (Core UI)** có thể chạy song song với bước 2: cài tarball utils local bằng `npm pack` + `npm install --no-save`, chỉ để phát triển, không commit.
4. **Phase 8 chỉ chạy khi `npm view @sdcorejs/utils@1.3.0 version` trả về `1.3.0`:**
   - TASK-018 pin từ registry;
   - TASK-019 sync và cài lại lockfile;
   - chạy lại toàn bộ lệnh kiểm chứng trên cây cuối cùng, để mọi bằng chứng gắn với lockfile thật.

## Frontend architecture plan

**Quy ước đã kiểm:**
- Component standalone, signal, control flow mới.
- Service root lấy qua `inject()`.
- Hàm thuần nhận collaborator qua tham số, không DI.
- Mỗi entry point có `index.ts` + `ng-package.json`.
- Export nội bộ giữa các entry point mang tiền tố `ɵ`.
- `SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION` là value token ở root.
- Date adapter khai bằng class provider ở cấp component.
- Pipe inject theo mẫu của `format-number.pipe.ts`.

**Cây phụ thuộc:**

```text
SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION.timeZone (root)
  └─ SdDateTimeService (root, @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time)
       ├─ pipes: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime, sdTimeDifferent, viewDateTime (history)
       ├─ forms: sd-date, sd-date-range, sd-datetime  (+ SdStrictDateFnsAdapter.today(), adapter con của sd-datetime)
       └─ components: sd-table (filter/local/sort/aggregate/export), sd-import-excel, form-generic, sd-file-explorer
```

**Ranh giới:**
- Service là cổng duy nhất cho giá trị model (INV-003).
- Vật mang chỉ được tính trên field local (INV-004).
- `forms/**` và `pipes/**` import thẳng `@sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time` (INV-008).
- Model-facing control bật khi có zone (INV-009).
- Pipe tạo bằng `new` ngoài injection context thì chạy như chế độ local (INV-010).

**Không thêm component.** File mới gồm: entry point `services/date-time`, adapter con trong `forms/datetime`, và các file spec.

## Preflight trước khi sửa (execute-plan chạy)

- **Ở từng repo:** `git status --short`, diffstat staged/unstaged, file untracked, nhánh hiện tại, HEAD, `allowed_paths`/`prohibited_paths`.
- **Có file bẩn không liên quan:** hỏi người dùng chọn một trong ba:
  1. tiếp tục nhưng chỉ sửa file trong plan;
  2. cho phép sửa một số file bẩn;
  3. dừng để dọn trước.
- **Core UI:**
  - worktree đã có `versions/v19/node_modules`; so `package-lock.json` trước khi dùng;
  - cần thì `npm --prefix versions/v19 ci --legacy-peer-deps` và `npm --prefix showcase ci --legacy-peer-deps`.
- **Utils:**
  - tạo worktree mới;
  - `npm ci` ở root và ở `site/`.

## Tasks

### Phase 1 - @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 (sdcorejs-ultis, TDD)

- **TASK-001** `sdcorejs-ultis` (không phụ thuộc): RED: spec múi giờ cho DateUtilities (toFormat/parse/parseFrom/begin/end, isValidTimeZone, toZonedParts, fromZonedParts) và FilterUtilities (date-today, date-relative, toán hạng không có zone); suite 1.2.6 giữ nguyên
  - CREATE `src/fns/date.time-zone.spec.ts`
  - CREATE `src/fns/filter.time-zone.spec.ts`

## Verification

- **Utils** (trong `C:/wt/utils-time-zone`):
  - `TZ=UTC npm test && TZ=Asia/Bangkok npm test`;
  - `npm run validate`;
  - `npm --prefix site run validate`.
- **Core UI theo khu vực** (`versions/v19`, `npx ng test sdcorejs-angular --watch=false --browsers=ChromeHeadless --include=…`):
  - từng khu vực: service, pipes + history, forms, table, import-excel, form-generic, file-explorer;
  - một lần chạy hồi quy gộp mọi khu vực (AC-015).
- **Quét tĩnh:**
  - import của `services/date-time`;
  - không có import barrel `services` từ `forms`/`pipes`;
  - không có `@sdcorejs/utils/dist`;
  - không còn lời gọi `DateUtilities.(toFormat|begin|end|parseFrom|parse)` trong các file không phải form/pipe đã đụng.
- **Build và rollout:**
  - `npm --prefix versions/v19 run build`;
  - sau khi pin: `npm run sync && npm run check:sync`, `npm run test:collect-docs`, test registry Showcase, kiểm pin 1.3.0 ở mọi `package.json`/lockfile;
  - build v20/v21/v22; `npm run test:showcase-examples`;
  - eslint trên file đã đổi.
- **Host UTC:** Chrome trên Windows chạy theo múi giờ của OS (`Asia/Bangkok`). Bằng chứng trên host UTC lấy từ CI của PR (VAL-004).
- **Không có kiểm tra thủ công** nào là điều kiện qua gate.

## Rủi ro thực thi

- **Đường dẫn trong plan lệch với thay đổi thật.** Ví dụ: `api-data.ts` hay `function-entries-a.spec.ts` của utils không cần sửa, hoặc cần sửa thêm file gọi helper của table. → Dừng, lập plan revision mới (`supersedes`), không sửa ngoài `allowed_paths`.
- **Sync ghi lại ~1150 file v22 thành CRLF.** → Checkout lại các file chỉ khác EOL; file thay đổi thật đổi sang LF.
- **Showcase dùng lib cũ.** → Sau khi build lại lib, chạy `link:library` và xoá `showcase/.angular/cache`.
- **Utils chưa publish khi đến Phase 8.** → Dừng ở cuối Phase 7, báo người dùng; không pin tarball.

## Self-review

- **Bảng phụ thuộc và kiểm chứng:**
  - decision coverage revision 5 hợp lệ ở bước `plan`;
  - goal-backward không còn blocker sau vòng 1;
  - validation map có 23 dòng, đều tự động, D-030 với `boundary: none`;
  - `validateArchitectureDraftPlanHandoff` hợp lệ;
  - giả lập convergence (feature, phạm vi Core UI) ra `CONVERGED`.
- **Đường dẫn:** mọi đường dẫn CREATE chưa tồn tại, mọi đường dẫn EDIT đã tồn tại (utils kiểm theo `9eb3052c`). Không có file nào thuộc hai task.
- **Ngoài phạm vi:**
  - thay đổi dependency (pin `@sdcorejs/utils` 1.3.0, lockfile v19–v22, showcase) là có chủ đích, phê duyệt cùng plan này;
  - không đổi env, không có migration.
- **API consistency:** đã đối chiếu và chốt trong spec r3 và architecture. Tên service theo `DateUtilities`, configuration đi qua `SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION`, kiểu dùng union literal. Review độc lập của phần code vẫn **pending**, làm ở bước review sau khi thực thi.

```yaml
plan_context:
  schema_version: 2
  source: sdcorejs-plan
  architecture_gate:
    valid: true
    required: true
    status: required
    signals:
      - conflicting-independent-unit-decisions
      - cross-repository-boundary
      - public-api-contract
    bypass: null
    rationale: >-
      Adds public API in two repositories (@sdcorejs/utils options and helpers; Core UI ISdCoreConfiguration.timeZone, SdTimeZone and SdDateTimeService), makes
      Core UI depend on a new utils minor, and about fifteen independent date surfaces must apply the same wall-clock semantics.
  architecture_context:
    schema_version: 1
    source: sdcorejs-architecture
    contract_id: core-ui-fixed-timezone
    requirement_id: R-003
    approved_spec_reference:
      repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
      artifact_id: spec-core-ui-fixed-timezone-r3
      artifact_kind: spec
      revision: 5f692cd3497a2a1ffa2e2c11d8a358b42476f799
      approval_hash: sha256:v1:ee30a3ada737f889bda593f0f5674d63d25c37a2e23de1ad065b37907eede067
    approved_architecture_path: .sdcorejs/architecture/angular/2026-10-16-15-55-core-ui-fixed-timezone.md
    approved_architecture_hash: sha256:v1:4d194a5e8ce45efd229b247c3b0433a639f5035c044e8127e14b530a81b1d217
    owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
    owner_module_id: null
    execution_host_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
    integration_owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
    trigger:
      required: true
      signals:
        - conflicting-independent-unit-decisions
        - cross-repository-boundary
        - public-api-contract
      rationale: >-
        Adds public API in two repositories (@sdcorejs/utils options and helpers; Core UI ISdCoreConfiguration.timeZone, SdTimeZone and SdDateTimeService),
        makes Core UI depend on a new utils minor, and about fifteen independent date surfaces must apply the same wall-clock semantics.
    invariants:
      - id: INV-001
        statement: >-
          Every @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 date and filter function called without a timeZone option returns exactly what 1.2.6 returns; zoned results depend only on
          the arguments (a per-zone Intl formatter cache is allowed, no other module state).
        scope: sdcorejs-ultis src/fns/date.fns.ts, src/fns/filter.fns.ts
        owner: sdcorejs-ultis/src/fns
        rationale: Backends and older callers rely on 1.2.6 results; options must be strictly additive.
        verification_method: VAL-001
        requirement_refs:
          - R-001
        decision_refs:
          - D-001
          - D-008
      - id: INV-002
        statement: >-
          @sdcorejs/angular imports @sdcorejs/utils only through its public subpaths, and every package.json and lockfile listed in the spec pins exactly 1.3.0;
          @sdcorejs/utils has no dependency on Angular or @sdcorejs/angular.
        scope: package pins, lockfiles and imports of @sdcorejs/utils
        owner: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: One-way dependency keeps utils framework-free and every workspace installable.
        verification_method: VAL-002
        requirement_refs:
          - R-001
          - R-010
        decision_refs:
          - D-001
          - D-013
          - D-023
      - id: INV-003
        statement: >-
          Inside @sdcorejs/angular every conversion of a model value (instant, zone-less string, date-only string, consumer Date) to display text, calendar day,
          wall clock, filter bound or serialized instant goes through SdDateTimeService; outside services/date-time no library file applies
          DateUtilities.toFormat/begin/end/parseFrom/parse, new Date(<value>), Date.parse, local getters or Intl.DateTimeFormat without timeZone to a model
          value.
        scope: every Core UI surface that handles a model date value
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/services/date-time
        rationale: A single gateway is the only way twenty surfaces stay consistent; one missed call reintroduces the 1960 shift.
        verification_method: VAL-003
        requirement_refs:
          - R-003
          - R-004
          - R-005
          - R-006
          - R-007
          - R-008
        decision_refs:
          - D-005
          - D-007
          - D-019
          - D-027
      - id: INV-004
        statement: >-
          Wall-clock carriers (picker and control Date objects, date-fns parsed control values, sd-chip-calendar cells, min/max carriers) are handled only with
          local-field arithmetic; the zone is applied exactly once per value, at the model boundary, and never to a carrier.
        scope: sd-date, sd-date-range, sd-datetime, picker adapters, sd-chip-calendar, forms/models
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/forms
        rationale: Material and the adapters only understand local Date objects; converting a carrier again shifts it by the zone difference.
        verification_method: VAL-004
        requirement_refs:
          - R-005
          - R-009
        decision_refs:
          - D-006
          - D-019
          - D-020
          - D-028
      - id: INV-005
        statement: >-
          With timeZone unset or local, SdDateTimeService calls DateUtilities without options (beginOfNextDay = begin(addDays(value, 1))) and every Core UI
          output, including the value registered in a parent form, equals the current release output.
        scope: SdDateTimeService local branch and every surface using it
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/services/date-time
        rationale: The feature is opt-in; consumers without timeZone must not see any change.
        verification_method: VAL-005
        requirement_refs:
          - R-002
          - R-009
        decision_refs:
          - D-005
          - D-021
      - id: INV-006
        statement: >-
          The effective zone is local or an id accepted by isValidTimeZone; numeric offsets and unknown ids make the SdDateTimeService constructor throw; the
          zone is resolved once from the root injector.
        scope: SdDateTimeService construction
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/services/date-time
        rationale: A fixed offset or a typo would silently reproduce the bug.
        verification_method: VAL-006
        requirement_refs:
          - R-002
        decision_refs:
          - D-002
          - D-003
          - D-009
          - D-022
      - id: INV-007
        statement: >-
          Calendar-day bounds are computed with calendar arithmetic in the zone (begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day), never by adding 24 hours; a
          day filter is [begin, beginOfNextDay).
        scope: utils begin/end and Core UI day filters
        owner: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: Days around offset changes are not 24 hours long (1975-06-12 in Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh has 25).
        verification_method: VAL-007
        requirement_refs:
          - R-001
          - R-006
        decision_refs:
          - D-008
          - D-021
      - id: INV-008
        statement: >-
          services/date-time depends only on @sdcorejs/angular/configurations and @sdcorejs/utils; forms/** and pipes/** import
          @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly and never the services barrel; services/date-time imports no pipes, forms, components or modules entry
          point.
        scope: imports of services/date-time and of the services barrel
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/services/date-time
        rationale: services/confirm imports forms/date and forms/datetime; a barrel import from forms would cycle.
        verification_method: VAL-002
        requirement_refs:
          - R-003
        decision_refs:
          - D-007
          - D-026
      - id: INV-009
        statement: >-
          With a configured zone, the control that sd-date, sd-datetime and sd-date-range register in a parent form carries the model value; without a zone it
          carries exactly today's value (sd-date: Date, sd-datetime: display string, sd-date-range: { from: Date, to: Date }).
        scope: model-facing registration of sd-date, sd-datetime, sd-date-range
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/forms
        rationale: A carrier in form.value serializes to a host-dependent instant.
        verification_method: VAL-008
        requirement_refs:
          - R-005
        decision_refs:
          - D-014
          - D-024
      - id: INV-010
        statement: The date pipes work with and without an injection context; without one they behave exactly as the local mode.
        scope: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime, sdTimeDifferent, viewDateTime construction
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/pipes
        rationale: Existing specs and consumers create these pipes with new.
        verification_method: VAL-009
        requirement_refs:
          - R-009
        decision_refs:
          - D-016
          - D-025
    boundaries:
      - id: B-001
        statement: >-
          @sdcorejs/utils owns zone arithmetic (Intl-based parts, zoned parse/format, calendar bounds, filter today/relative); Core UI owns the configuration
          and the Angular wiring.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-001
          - INV-002
      - id: B-002
        statement: SdDateTimeService is the single gateway between model date values and the configured zone inside Core UI.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-003
          - INV-005
          - INV-006
      - id: B-003
        statement: >-
          The model<->control boundary of each date form (normalize, writeValue, commit/serialize, min/max, model-facing registration) is the only place where a
          model value becomes a carrier and back.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-004
          - INV-009
    dependency_directions:
      - from: sdcorejs-angular (all lines, showcase)
        to: '@sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 (exact pin, public subpaths only)'
        rationale: Core UI consumes TimeZoneOptions, parse, zoned parts and FilterUtilities timeZone.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-002
      - from: services/date-time
        to: configurations, @sdcorejs/utils/fns
        rationale: Reads SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION.timeZone and delegates the arithmetic.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-008
      - from: >-
          pipes, forms/models, forms/date, forms/date-range, forms/datetime, components/table, components/import-excel, components/form-generic,
          components/history, components/file-explorer
        to: services/date-time (direct entry point, never the services barrel)
        rationale: Every model date conversion goes through the service.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-003
          - INV-008
    data_state_owners:
      - subject: System time zone (root SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION.timeZone), resolved once by the root SdDateTimeService
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-006
      - subject: IANA tzdata (browser Intl / ICU)
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-ultis
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-001
    public_contracts:
      - id: PC-001
        kind: api
        owner: sdcorejs-ultis/src/fns
        statement: >-
          @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0: DateUtilities TimeZoneOptions on toFormat/parseFrom/begin/end, parse, isValidTimeZone, toZonedParts, fromZonedParts,
          ZonedDateTimeParts; FilterUtilities MatchOptions.timeZone, ToEpochOptions.timeZone, resolveRelativeDate(relative, now, options).
        compatibility: Additive minor; calls without options are unchanged (INV-001).
        migration: None. Callers opt in by passing { timeZone }.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-001
          - INV-007
      - id: PC-002
        kind: api
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/configurations
        statement: 'ISdCoreConfiguration.timeZone?: SdTimeZone and the exported SdTimeZone open union, read from the root injector.'
        compatibility: Additive; default local keeps current behaviour (INV-005).
        migration: 'Opt in with { provide: SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION, useValue: { ..., timeZone: ''Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh'' } } at the root.'
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-005
          - INV-006
      - id: PC-003
        kind: api
        owner: sdcorejs-angular/services/date-time
        statement: >-
          New entry point @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time exporting SdDateTimeService (timeZone, toFormat, parse, parseFrom, begin, end, beginOfNextDay,
          today, now, toWallClock, fromWallClock), re-exported by @sdcorejs/angular/services.
        compatibility: New entry point; nothing removed.
        migration: App code that formats or parses model dates may switch from DateUtilities to SdDateTimeService to follow the system zone.
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-003
          - INV-006
          - INV-008
      - id: PC-004
        kind: api
        owner: sdcorejs-angular
        statement: >-
          Under a configured zone: transform outputs of the date forms, table filter payloads and import-excel instants are computed in that zone, and
          form.value of sd-date/sd-datetime/sd-date-range holds the model value instead of a Date.
        compatibility: Changes only for apps that set timeZone; documented in the CHANGELOG and component docs.
        migration: Apps that set timeZone and read form.value as Date switch to the model string (or use transform for an ISO instant).
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-003
          - INV-004
          - INV-007
          - INV-009
    security_trust_boundaries: []
    cross_repository_integration:
      - owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        statement: sdcorejs-ultis delivers @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 (merge + publish) before the Core UI change pins it (D-023).
        child_references:

      - id: VAL-002

        invariant_refs:

        expected_proof: >-
          Every package.json and lockfile listed in the spec pins 1.3.0; grep finds no @sdcorejs/utils/dist import, no services-barrel import under forms/** or
          pipes/**, and no pipes/forms/components/modules import under services/date-time; ng build sdcorejs-angular in v19 and check:sync pass.
      - id: VAL-003

      - id: VAL-004

        invariant_refs:

        acceptance_criterion_refs:

          - AC-009
          - AC-010

        acceptance_criterion_refs:

          - AC-006
        expected_proof: 'SdDateTimeService spec: invalid values throw the documented message, valid values resolve once, methods return the AC-006 values.'
      - id: VAL-007

        invariant_refs:
          - INV-007
        acceptance_criterion_refs:

          - AC-011
          - AC-012
        expected_proof: The 1975-06-12 Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh cases (25-hour day) pass in utils and in the sd-table server and client filter specs.
      - id: VAL-008
        owner: sdcorejs-angular
        invariant_refs:
          - INV-009
        acceptance_criterion_refs:

        expected_proof: Form specs bind the three components to a FormGroup and assert the model value under a zone and the Date without one.

  decision_coverage:
    schema_version: 1
    revision: 5
    records:
      - id: R-001
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 (minor): DateUtilities gains TimeZoneOptions ({ timeZone?: string }, an IANA id) as the last optional argument of toFormat,
          parseFrom, begin and end, a new public lenient parse(value, options?) returning Date | null for every input isDate accepts, plus isValidTimeZone,
          toZonedParts and fromZonedParts; FilterUtilities gains timeZone in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions so date-today, date-relative and zone-less date
          operands resolve in that zone, and resolveRelativeDate accepts the same option; calls without the option behave exactly as 1.2.6; no global or
          module-level state; the dates-time-zones guide, README and changeset describe the API.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-ultis
        owner_module_id: src/fns/date
        task_refs:
          - TASK-001
          - TASK-002
          - TASK-003
      - id: R-002
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          ISdCoreConfiguration gains timeZone?: SdTimeZone, an open literal union of 'local', common IANA ids and any other IANA id, read from the root injector
          only; unset or 'local' keeps the current browser-local behavior; a numeric offset or unknown id throws a clear Error on the first use of the date/time
          service.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: configurations
        task_refs:
          - TASK-004
          - TASK-005
          - TASK-010
          - TASK-011
          - TASK-012
          - TASK-013
          - TASK-014
      - id: R-003
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time exports SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) bound to the configured zone: timeZone, toFormat, parse, parseFrom,
          begin, end, beginOfNextDay, today, now, toWallClock and fromWallClock.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: services/date-time
        task_refs:
          - TASK-004
          - TASK-005
      - id: R-004
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          Display surfaces format an instant as the wall clock of the configured zone: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime and sdTimeDifferent (and through them
          sd-table cells and aggregate MIN/MAX), the read-only text of sd-date-range (query-bar chips), history viewDateTime, form-generic read-only display and
          builder inspector default display, the import-excel column transform pipe, and file-explorer modifiedAt including its today/yesterday labels.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: pipes
        task_refs:
          - TASK-006
          - TASK-007
          - TASK-008
          - TASK-009
          - TASK-010
          - TASK-011
          - TASK-012
          - TASK-013
          - TASK-014
      - id: R-005
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          sd-date, sd-date-range and sd-datetime show an instant model as the calendar day or wall clock of the configured zone, keep picker and control Date
          objects as wall-clock carriers, serialize transform (ISOString/UTCString) from that wall clock in the zone (00:00 of the day for date inputs), resolve
          TODAY, min/max bounds and the date adapter today() (calendar today marker, Now button) in the zone, and, when a zone is configured, register the model
          value instead of the carrier in a parent form even without transform.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: forms
        task_refs:
          - TASK-008
          - TASK-009
      - id: R-006
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          sd-table server filters (date, daterange, datetime and range payloads), client-side filtering (row values parsed through the service), local sort,
          aggregate parsing and Excel export compute in the configured zone: a day filter is [begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day) in the zone and
          a datetime filter reads the zone-less value as wall clock in the zone; in local mode every bound equals the current value. Row-value parsing at the
          client filter, local sort, aggregate and min/max sites also stays exactly as today in local mode.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: components/table
        task_refs:
          - TASK-010
          - TASK-011
      - id: R-007
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          With a configured zone, import-excel reads Date cells (exceljs UTC fields = Excel wall clock) and text dates parsed with the column format (time-only
          formats use today in the zone) as wall clock in the zone; the stored types stay as today (Date cell: ISO string, text cell: Date) and unset keeps the
          current values.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: components/import-excel
        task_refs:
          - TASK-012
      - id: R-008
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          form-generic resolves the today min/max boundary of datetime fields to the start/end of today in the configured zone, evaluates its rules and
          validations with FilterUtilities MatchOptions.timeZone, and formats its read-only display through a date formatter passed in its display options.
        source: explicit-user
        status: active
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: components/form-generic
        task_refs:
          - TASK-013
      - id: R-009
        type: requirement
        statement: >-
          Calendar-only values and wall-clock carriers never shift: date-only strings, zone-less wall-clock strings emitted without transform, sd-chip-calendar
          values, forms/time values and picker/control Date objects show and emit the same digits under every configuration; pipes created with new outside an
          injection context keep the local behavior. sdParseTransformedTemporal keeps its current public behavior.
        source: explicit-user

        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        owner_module_id: forms

          that set timeZone: sd-date Date -> 'yyyy/MM/dd', sd-datetime display string -> 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', sd-date-range { from: Date, to: Date } -> the
          range model) describe timeZone; the Showcase registers sd-date-time; every Angular line, every versions/v* lockfile and the showcase pin

          the repeated 23:30 of 1975-06-12 resolves to the earlier offset 1975-06-12T15:30:00.000Z; begin/end of 1975/06/12 are 1975-06-11T16:00:00.000Z and

        expected_result: >-

          'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh'
        expected_result: with the zone today resolves to 2026-08-14T17:00:00.000Z and the item matches; without the zone the result equals 1.2.6
        verification_kind: automated
        blocking: true
        requirement_refs:
          - R-001
        task_refs:

      - id: A-001
        type: assumption
        statement: >-
          Every browser supported by Angular 19-22 implements Intl.DateTimeFormat timeZone with IANA history (ICU tzdata), including Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh +08 for
          1959-12-31..1975-06-13.
        source: inferred
        confidence: high
        status: confirmed
        blocking: false
        evidence_refs: []
        consequence_if_wrong: A browser with stale tzdata would convert 1960-1975 values with the wrong offset.
        validation_method: Karma (ChromeHeadless) specs assert 1960 and 1975 values; Node 22 Intl gives the same offsets.
        owner: sdcorejs-angular maintainers
        rationale: Intl timeZone support is ES2017 baseline; verified with Node 22 Intl on 2026-10-16.
        impacted_refs:
          - R-001
          - R-003
      - id: A-002
        type: assumption

        confidence: medium
        status: confirmed
        blocking: false
        evidence_refs: []
        consequence_if_wrong: Imported Date cells would shift by the host offset under a configured zone.
        validation_method: An import-excel spec reads a workbook with a real date cell through the excel service.
        owner: sdcorejs-angular maintainers
        rationale: exceljs converts the Excel serial with UTC arithmetic; to be confirmed by the spec.
        impacted_refs:

          parse/parseFrom/begin/end/beginOfNextDay/toWallClock return null and toFormat returns an empty string for invalid input; in local mode beginOfNextDay
          is exactly DateUtilities.begin(DateUtilities.addDays(value, 1))
        question: Public shape of the Core UI service

      - id: D-008
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Public shape of the utils additions: DateUtilities: TimeZoneOptions { timeZone?: string } as the last optional argument of toFormat, parseFrom, begin
          and end; new parse(value, options?) returning Date | null; isValidTimeZone(value); toZonedParts(value, timeZone) returning { year, month (1-12), day,
          hour, minute, second, millisecond } or null; fromZonedParts(parts, timeZone) normalizing out-of-range fields like Date.UTC; FilterUtilities: timeZone
          in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions, resolveRelativeDate(relative, now, options?); a repeated wall clock resolves to the earlier offset and a skipped
          one moves forward (Temporal 'compatible'); with timeZone, zone-less and date-only strings are read from their own fields (never round-tripped through
          an instant), so their digits never change
        question: Public shape of the utils additions
        selected_value: >-

          or null; fromZonedParts(parts, timeZone) normalizing out-of-range fields like Date.UTC; FilterUtilities: timeZone in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions,
          resolveRelativeDate(relative, now, options?); a repeated wall clock resolves to the earlier offset and a skipped one moves forward (Temporal
          'compatible'); with timeZone, zone-less and date-only strings are read from their own fields (never round-tripped through an instant), so their digits
          never change

          (RFC-1123, ISO with a space separator plus Z/offset, more than 9 fraction digits, surrounding whitespace) ; the public function keeps its current
          behavior and the forms use an internal instant-only variant (null for zone-less matches) only when a zone is configured. A model Date is an instant
        question: How a form resolves its model

          carriers keep local helpers. sdParseTransformedTemporal is a fallback only for what utils rejects (RFC-1123, ISO with a space separator plus Z/offset,
          more than 9 fraction digits, surrounding whitespace) ; the public function keeps its current behavior and the forms use an internal instant-only
          variant (null for zone-less matches) only when a zone is configured. A model Date is an instant

      - id: D-021
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Exclusive upper bound of a day filter: service.beginOfNextDay(day) replaces begin(addDays(day, 1)) at every LESS_THAN day bound; in local mode it
          calls exactly begin(addDays(day, 1)), including its null result, so callers keep their current null handling; range payloads keep the inclusive
          end(day)
        question: Exclusive upper bound of a day filter
        selected_value: >-
          service.beginOfNextDay(day) replaces begin(addDays(day, 1)) at every LESS_THAN day bound; in local mode it calls exactly begin(addDays(day, 1)),
          including its null result, so callers keep their current null handling; range payloads keep the inclusive end(day)
        source: approved-architecture
        status: approved
        blocking: true
        scope: repository
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: The review measured end + 1 ms differing from the current bound on offset-change days in the host zone; local mode must stay byte-identical.
        supersedes: null
        revisit_condition: null
        convention_impact:
          candidate: false
          category: null
        downstream_refs:
          - R-006
          - AC-011
          - AC-015

        task_refs:

          - TASK-010
          - TASK-011
      - id: D-022
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Lifetime of the configured zone: SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) reads the root SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION once in its constructor and validates it
          there; providers of SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION in lazy or element injectors do not change the zone
        question: Lifetime of the configured zone
        selected_value: >-
          SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) reads the root SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION once in its constructor and validates it there; providers of
          SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION in lazy or element injectors do not change the zone
        source: approved-architecture
        status: approved
        blocking: true
        scope: repository
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: One resolution keeps every surface consistent; documented next to format.number, which element injectors can override.
        supersedes: null
        revisit_condition: null
        convention_impact:
          candidate: false
          category: null
        downstream_refs:

          - AC-005

      - id: D-023
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Cross-repository delivery order: sdcorejs-ultis merges and publishes @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 first; the Core UI change commits only registry pins and
          lockfiles for 1.3.0; a local npm pack tarball may be used while iterating and is never committed
        question: Cross-repository delivery order
        selected_value: >-
          sdcorejs-ultis merges and publishes @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 first; the Core UI change commits only registry pins and lockfiles for 1.3.0; a local npm
          pack tarball may be used while iterating and is never committed
        source: approved-architecture
        status: approved
        blocking: true
        scope: repository
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: Core UI CI installs from the registry and the release install policy forbids local tarballs.
        supersedes: null
        revisit_condition: null
        convention_impact:
          candidate: false
          category: null
        downstream_refs:
          - R-001

          - AC-017

        task_refs:

          - TASK-019
      - id: D-024
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          How the parent form receives the model under a zone: The existing ɵsdModelFacingControl of sd-date, sd-datetime and sd-date-range becomes active when
          transform is set or a zone is configured (service.timeZone !== local)
        question: How the parent form receives the model under a zone
        selected_value: >-
          The existing ɵsdModelFacingControl of sd-date, sd-datetime and sd-date-range becomes active when transform is set or a zone is configured
          (service.timeZone !== local)
        source: approved-architecture
        status: approved
        blocking: true
        scope: repository
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: Reuses the mechanism that already keeps form.get(name).value equal to the model for transform.
        supersedes: null
        revisit_condition: null
        convention_impact:
          candidate: false
          category: null
        downstream_refs:

          - INV-009

          - TASK-009
      - id: D-025
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          How pipes reach the service: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime, sdTimeDifferent and viewDateTime call the public assertInInjectionContext inside a try
          (only NG0203 is caught, then DateUtilities without options is used) and call inject(SdDateTimeService) outside the try, so a service error such as an
          invalid timeZone still propagates; new inside a constructor or field initializer runs in an injection context and follows the zone (documented)
        question: How pipes reach the service
        selected_value: >-
          sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime, sdTimeDifferent and viewDateTime call the public assertInInjectionContext inside a try (only NG0203 is caught, then
          DateUtilities without options is used) and call inject(SdDateTimeService) outside the try, so a service error such as an invalid timeZone still
          propagates; new inside a constructor or field initializer runs in an injection context and follows the zone (documented)
        source: approved-architecture
        status: approved
        blocking: true
        scope: repository
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: Keeps new SdFormatDatePipe() working (D-016) without a global.
        supersedes: null
        revisit_condition: null
        convention_impact:

          category: null
        downstream_refs:

          - TASK-007
      - id: D-026
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Import path of the service inside the library: forms/** and pipes/** (and every entry reachable from services/confirm) import
          @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly, never the @sdcorejs/angular/services barrel
        question: Import path of the service inside the library
        selected_value: >-
          forms/** and pipes/** (and every entry reachable from services/confirm) import @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly, never the
          @sdcorejs/angular/services barrel
        source: approved-architecture
        status: approved
        blocking: true
        scope: repository
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: The barrel re-exports services/confirm, which imports forms/date and forms/datetime; importing the barrel from forms would create an ng-packagr cycle.
        supersedes: null

      - id: D-027
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Form-generic wiring: sdDisplayValue stays pure and receives a date formatter through SdFormGenericDisplayOptions (like numberFormat); rule and
          validation evaluation pass { timeZone } to FilterUtilities only when a zone is configured
        question: Form-generic wiring
        selected_value: >-
          sdDisplayValue stays pure and receives a date formatter through SdFormGenericDisplayOptions (like numberFormat); rule and validation evaluation pass {
          timeZone } to FilterUtilities only when a zone is configured
        source: approved-architecture
        status: approved
        blocking: true
        scope: repository
        owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
        rationale: Pure functions cannot inject; passing nothing in local mode keeps 1.2.6 results.

      - id: D-028
        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Min/max and TODAY bounds of the date forms: #parseDateBoundary goes through the service: 'TODAY' is today() for dates and now() for datetimes; other
          values become carriers with toWallClock (datetime) or the zone calendar day (date); without a zone #parseDateBoundary keeps its current code (D-029)
        question: Min/max and TODAY bounds of the date forms
        selected_value: >-
          #parseDateBoundary goes through the service: 'TODAY' is today() for dates and now() for datetimes; other values become carriers with toWallClock
          (datetime) or the zone calendar day (date); without a zone #parseDateBoundary keeps its current code (D-029)
        source: approved-architecture

        type: decision
        statement: >-
          Sites that parse with new Date(...) today: table-local.util (client filter), table-local-sort.util, table-aggregate.util and #parseDateBoundary of the
          three forms keep new Date(...) in local mode and use service.parse only when a zone is configured
        question: Sites that parse with new Date(...) today
        selected_value: >-
          table-local.util (client filter), table-local-sort.util, table-aggregate.util and #parseDateBoundary of the three forms keep new Date(...) in local
          mode and use service.parse only when a zone is configured
        source: approved-architecture

          @sdcorejs/utils has no dependency on Angular or @sdcorejs/angular.

          Inside @sdcorejs/angular every conversion of a model value (instant, zone-less string, date-only string, consumer Date) to display text, calendar day,
          wall clock, filter bound or serialized instant goes through SdDateTimeService; outside services/date-time no library file applies
          DateUtilities.toFormat/begin/end/parseFrom/parse, new Date(<value>), Date.parse, local getters or Intl.DateTimeFormat without timeZone to a model
          value.

        statement: >-

        1.3.0 (minor): DateUtilities gains TimeZoneOptions ({ timeZone?: string }, an IANA id) as the last optional argument of toFormat, parseFrom, begin and
        end, a new public lenient parse(value, options?) returning Date | null for every input isDate accepts, plus isValidTimeZone, toZonedParts and
        fromZonedParts; FilterUtilities gains timeZone in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions so date-today, date-relative and zone-less date operands resolve in
        that zone, and resolveRelativeDate accepts the same option; calls without the option behave exactly as 1.2.6; no global or module-level state; the
        dates-time-zones guide, README and changeset describe the
        API.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-001","TASK-002","TASK-003"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-002","owner_module_id":"configurations","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"ISdCoreConfiguration
        gains timeZone?: SdTimeZone, an open literal union of 'local', common IANA ids and any other IANA id, read from the root injector only; unset or 'local'
        keeps the current browser-local behavior; a numeric offset or unknown id throws a clear Error on the first use of the date/time
        service.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-003","owner_module_id":"services/date-time","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"@sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time
        exports SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) bound to the configured zone: timeZone, toFormat, parse, parseFrom, begin, end, beginOfNextDay, today, now,
        toWallClock and
        fromWallClock.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-004","owner_module_id":"pipes","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"Display
        surfaces format an instant as the wall clock of the configured zone: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime and sdTimeDifferent (and through them sd-table cells
        and aggregate MIN/MAX), the read-only text of sd-date-range (query-bar chips), history viewDateTime, form-generic read-only display and builder
        inspector default display, the import-excel column transform pipe, and file-explorer modifiedAt including its today/yesterday
        labels.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-008","TASK-009","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-005","owner_module_id":"forms","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"sd-date,
        sd-date-range and sd-datetime show an instant model as the calendar day or wall clock of the configured zone, keep picker and control Date objects as
        wall-clock carriers, serialize transform (ISOString/UTCString) from that wall clock in the zone (00:00 of the day for date inputs), resolve TODAY,
        min/max bounds and the date adapter today() (calendar today marker, Now button) in the zone, and, when a zone is configured, register the model value
        instead of the carrier in a parent form even without
        transform.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-006","owner_module_id":"components/table","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"sd-table
        server filters (date, daterange, datetime and range payloads), client-side filtering (row values parsed through the service), local sort, aggregate
        parsing and Excel export compute in the configured zone: a day filter is [begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day) in the zone and a datetime
        filter reads the zone-less value as wall clock in the zone; in local mode every bound equals the current value. Row-value parsing at the client filter,
        local sort, aggregate and min/max sites also stays exactly as today in local
        mode.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-010","TASK-011"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-007","owner_module_id":"components/import-excel","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"With
        a configured zone, import-excel reads Date cells (exceljs UTC fields = Excel wall clock) and text dates parsed with the column format (time-only formats
        use today in the zone) as wall clock in the zone; the stored types stay as today (Date cell: ISO string, text cell: Date) and unset keeps the current
        values.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-012"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-008","owner_module_id":"components/form-generic","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"form-generic
        resolves the today min/max boundary of datetime fields to the start/end of today in the configured zone, evaluates its rules and validations with
        FilterUtilities MatchOptions.timeZone, and formats its read-only display through a date formatter passed in its display
        options.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-013"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-009","owner_module_id":"forms","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"Calendar-only
        values and wall-clock carriers never shift: date-only strings, zone-less wall-clock strings emitted without transform, sd-chip-calendar values,
        forms/time values and picker/control Date objects show and emit the same digits under every configuration; pipes created with new outside an injection
        context keep the local behavior. sdParseTransformedTemporal keeps its current public
        behavior.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-010","owner_module_id":null,"owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"Core
        UI docs (sd-core.configuration, new sd-date-time, pipes, sd-date, sd-datetime, sd-date-range, sd-table filters, sd-import-excel, sd-file-explorer,
        sd-form-generic, query-bar/query-builder backend notes) and the CHANGELOG Unreleased entry (including the form.value change for apps that set timeZone:
        sd-date Date -> 'yyyy/MM/dd', sd-datetime display string -> 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', sd-date-range { from: Date, to: Date } -> the range model) describe
        timeZone; the Showcase registers sd-date-time; every Angular line, every versions/v* lockfile and the showcase pin @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0; v20-v22
        sources come only from npm run
        sync.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-015","TASK-016","TASK-017","TASK-018","TASK-019"],"type":"requirement"},{"behavior":"Call

        of the configured zone","rationale":"One resolution keeps every surface consistent; documented next to format.number, which element injectors can
        override.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) reads the root SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION once
        in its constructor and validates it there; providers of SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION in lazy or element injectors do not change the
        zone","source":"approved-architecture","statement":"Lifetime of the configured zone: SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) reads the root
        SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION once in its constructor and validates it there; providers of SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION in lazy or element injectors do not change the
        zone","status":"approved","supersedes":null,"task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005","TASK-015"],"type":"decision"},{"blocking":true,"convention_impact":{"candidate":false,"category":null},"downstream_refs":["R-001","R-010","AC-017","INV-002"],"id":"D-023","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","question":"Cross-repository
        delivery order","rationale":"Core UI CI installs from the registry and the release install policy forbids local

        committed","source":"approved-architecture","statement":"Cross-repository delivery order: sdcorejs-ultis merges and publishes @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0
        first; the Core UI change commits only registry pins and lockfiles for 1.3.0; a local npm pack tarball may be used while iterating and is never
        committed","status":"approved","supersedes":null,"task_refs":["TASK-003","TASK-018","TASK-019"],"type":"decision"},{"blocking":true,"convention_impact":{"candidate":false,"category":null},"downstream_refs":["R-005","AC-018","INV-009"],"id":"D-024","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","question":"How
        the parent form receives the model under a zone","rationale":"Reuses the mechanism that already keeps form.get(name).value equal to the model for
        transform.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"The existing ɵsdModelFacingControl of sd-date, sd-datetime and sd-date-range

        pipes reach the service","rationale":"Keeps new SdFormatDatePipe() working (D-016) without a
        global.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime, sdTimeDifferent and viewDateTime call the
        public assertInInjectionContext inside a try (only NG0203 is caught, then DateUtilities without options is used) and call inject(SdDateTimeService)

        context and follows the zone (documented)","source":"approved-architecture","statement":"How pipes reach the service: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime,
        sdTimeDifferent and viewDateTime call the public assertInInjectionContext inside a try (only NG0203 is caught, then DateUtilities without options is

        field initializer runs in an injection context and follows the zone
        (documented)","status":"approved","supersedes":null,"task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007"],"type":"decision"},{"blocking":true,"convention_impact":{"candidate":false,"category":null},"downstream_refs":["R-003","AC-017","INV-008"],"id":"D-026","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","question":"Import
        path of the service inside the library","rationale":"The barrel re-exports services/confirm, which imports forms/date and forms/datetime; importing the
        barrel from forms would create an ng-packagr cycle.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"forms/** and pipes/** (and every

        results.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"sdDisplayValue stays pure and receives a date formatter through
        SdFormGenericDisplayOptions (like numberFormat); rule and validation evaluation pass { timeZone } to FilterUtilities only when a zone is

        and TODAY bounds of the date forms","rationale":"The review found bounds built with new Date(value), bypassing the model
        boundary.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"#parseDateBoundary goes through the service: 'TODAY' is today() for dates and

        service: 'TODAY' is today() for dates and now() for datetimes; other values become carriers with toWallClock (datetime) or the zone calendar day (date);
        without a zone #parseDateBoundary keeps its current code
        (D-029)","status":"approved","supersedes":null,"task_refs":["TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"decision"},{"blocking":true,"convention_impact":{"candidate":false,"category":null},"downstream_refs":["R-006","AC-012","AC-015","INV-005"],"id":"D-029","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","question":"Sites
        that parse with new Date(...) today","rationale":"Native parsing and utils parsing differ for yyyy-MM-dd, numbers, RFC-1123 and Date.toString text;
        switching them in local mode would break INV-005.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"table-local.util (client filter),
        table-local-sort.util, table-aggregate.util and #parseDateBoundary of the three forms keep new Date(...) in local mode and use service.parse only when a

        table-local-sort.util, table-aggregate.util and #parseDateBoundary of the three forms keep new Date(...) in local mode and use service.parse only when a
        zone is
        configured","status":"approved","supersedes":null,"task_refs":["TASK-009","TASK-010","TASK-011"],"type":"decision"},{"blocking":true,"convention_impact":{"candidate":false,"category":null},"downstream_refs":["R-001","R-002","R-003","R-004","R-005","R-006","R-007","R-008","R-009","R-010","AC-001","AC-002","AC-003","AC-004","AC-005","AC-006","AC-007","AC-008","AC-009","AC-010","AC-011","AC-012","AC-013","AC-014","AC-015","AC-016","AC-017","AC-018","AC-019","AC-020","AC-021","AC-022","INV-001","INV-002","INV-003","INV-004","INV-005","INV-006","INV-007","INV-008","INV-009","INV-010"],"id":"D-030","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","question":"Ranh
        giới kiểm chứng nào áp dụng?","rationale":"Không có quyền hay endpoint server nào trong phạm vi; mọi bằng chứng là test tự động của thư
        viện.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"none","source":"approved-plan","statement":"Ranh giới kiểm chứng nào áp dụng? →
        none: thay đổi là thư viện UI/tiện ích phía client, không có ranh giới phân quyền hay API phía server trong phạm
        vi","status":"approved","supersedes":null,"task_refs":["TASK-019"],"type":"decision","validation_boundary":{"kind":"none","source_refs":["R-001","R-002","R-003","R-004","R-005","R-006","R-007","R-008","R-009","R-010","AC-001","AC-002","AC-003","AC-004","AC-005","AC-006","AC-007","AC-008","AC-009","AC-010","AC-011","AC-012","AC-013","AC-014","AC-015","AC-016","AC-017","AC-018","AC-019","AC-020","AC-021","AC-022","INV-001","INV-002","INV-003","INV-004","INV-005","INV-006","INV-007","INV-008","INV-009","INV-010"]}},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-001","EVIDENCE-002","EVIDENCE-003","EVIDENCE-004"],"id":"INV-001","protected_refs":["R-001","D-001","D-008","AC-001","AC-002","AC-003","AC-004","AC-022"],"statement":"Every
        @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 date and filter function called without a timeZone option returns exactly what 1.2.6 returns; zoned results depend only on the
        arguments (a per-zone Intl formatter cache is allowed, no other module
        state).","task_refs":["TASK-001","TASK-002","TASK-003"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-004","EVIDENCE-008","EVIDENCE-034","EVIDENCE-035","EVIDENCE-036","EVIDENCE-037","EVIDENCE-038","EVIDENCE-039","EVIDENCE-040","EVIDENCE-041"],"id":"INV-002","protected_refs":["R-001","R-010","D-001","D-013","D-023","AC-017"],"statement":"@sdcorejs/angular
        imports @sdcorejs/utils only through its public subpaths, and every package.json and lockfile listed in the spec pins exactly 1.3.0; @sdcorejs/utils has
        no dependency on Angular or
        @sdcorejs/angular.","task_refs":["TASK-003","TASK-005","TASK-015","TASK-016","TASK-017","TASK-018","TASK-019"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-010","EVIDENCE-012","EVIDENCE-017","EVIDENCE-020","EVIDENCE-021","EVIDENCE-023","EVIDENCE-024","EVIDENCE-026","EVIDENCE-027","EVIDENCE-029","EVIDENCE-030","EVIDENCE-032"],"id":"INV-003","protected_refs":["R-003","R-004","R-005","R-006","R-007","R-008","D-005","D-007","D-019","D-027","AC-007","AC-011","AC-012","AC-013","AC-014","AC-020","AC-021"],"statement":"Inside
        @sdcorejs/angular every conversion of a model value (instant, zone-less string, date-only string, consumer Date) to display text, calendar day, wall
        clock, filter bound or serialized instant goes through SdDateTimeService; outside services/date-time no library file applies
        DateUtilities.toFormat/begin/end/parseFrom/parse, new Date(<value>), Date.parse, local getters or Intl.DateTimeFormat without timeZone to a model
        value.","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-009","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-015","EVIDENCE-017"],"id":"INV-004","protected_refs":["R-005","R-009","D-006","D-019","D-020","D-028","AC-008","AC-009","AC-010","AC-016","AC-019"],"statement":"Wall-clock
        carriers (picker and control Date objects, date-fns parsed control values, sd-chip-calendar cells, min/max carriers) are handled only with local-field
        arithmetic; the zone is applied exactly once per value, at the model boundary, and never to a
        carrier.","task_refs":["TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-005","EVIDENCE-006","EVIDENCE-007","EVIDENCE-011","EVIDENCE-013","EVIDENCE-016","EVIDENCE-018","EVIDENCE-022","EVIDENCE-025","EVIDENCE-028","EVIDENCE-031","EVIDENCE-033"],"id":"INV-005","protected_refs":["R-002","R-009","D-005","D-021","AC-015"],"statement":"With
        timeZone unset or local, SdDateTimeService calls DateUtilities without options (beginOfNextDay = begin(addDays(value, 1))) and every Core UI output,
        including the value registered in a parent form, equals the current release
        output.","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005","TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-008","TASK-009","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-005","EVIDENCE-006"],"id":"INV-006","protected_refs":["R-002","D-002","D-003","D-009","D-022","AC-005","AC-006"],"statement":"The
        effective zone is local or an id accepted by isValidTimeZone; numeric offsets and unknown ids make the SdDateTimeService constructor throw; the zone is
        resolved once from the root
        injector.","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-001","EVIDENCE-002","EVIDENCE-003","EVIDENCE-020","EVIDENCE-023"],"id":"INV-007","protected_refs":["R-001","R-006","D-008","D-021","AC-002","AC-011","AC-012"],"statement":"Calendar-day
        bounds are computed with calendar arithmetic in the zone (begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day), never by adding 24 hours; a day filter is
        [begin,
        beginOfNextDay).","task_refs":["TASK-001","TASK-002","TASK-010","TASK-011"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-008","EVIDENCE-009","EVIDENCE-014","EVIDENCE-019"],"id":"INV-008","protected_refs":["R-003","D-007","D-026","AC-017"],"statement":"services/date-time
        depends only on @sdcorejs/angular/configurations and @sdcorejs/utils; forms/** and pipes/** import @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly and
        never the services barrel; services/date-time imports no pipes, forms, components or modules entry
        point.","task_refs":["TASK-005","TASK-007","TASK-009"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-015","EVIDENCE-016","EVIDENCE-017","EVIDENCE-018"],"id":"INV-009","protected_refs":["R-005","D-014","D-024","AC-018","AC-015"],"statement":"With
        a configured zone, the control that sd-date, sd-datetime and sd-date-range register in a parent form carries the model value; without a zone it carries
        exactly today's value (sd-date: Date, sd-datetime: display string, sd-date-range: { from: Date, to: Date
        }).","task_refs":["TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-010","EVIDENCE-011","EVIDENCE-012","EVIDENCE-013"],"id":"INV-010","protected_refs":["R-009","D-016","D-025","AC-015"],"statement":"The
        date pipes work with and without an injection context; without one they behave exactly as the local
        mode.","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007"],"type":"invariant"}],"revision":5,"schema_version":1}
  goal_backward_review:
    schema_version: 1
    mode: sdcorejs-plan:goal-backward
    decision_coverage:
      schema_version: 1
      revision: 5
      records:
        - id: R-001
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 (minor): DateUtilities gains TimeZoneOptions ({ timeZone?: string }, an IANA id) as the last optional argument of toFormat,
            parseFrom, begin and end, a new public lenient parse(value, options?) returning Date | null for every input isDate accepts, plus isValidTimeZone,
            toZonedParts and fromZonedParts; FilterUtilities gains timeZone in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions so date-today, date-relative and zone-less date
            operands resolve in that zone, and resolveRelativeDate accepts the same option; calls without the option behave exactly as 1.2.6; no global or
            module-level state; the dates-time-zones guide, README and changeset describe the API.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-ultis
          owner_module_id: src/fns/date
          task_refs:
            - TASK-001
            - TASK-002
            - TASK-003
        - id: R-002
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            ISdCoreConfiguration gains timeZone?: SdTimeZone, an open literal union of 'local', common IANA ids and any other IANA id, read from the root
            injector only; unset or 'local' keeps the current browser-local behavior; a numeric offset or unknown id throws a clear Error on the first use of
            the date/time service.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: configurations
          task_refs:
            - TASK-004
            - TASK-005
            - TASK-010
            - TASK-011
            - TASK-012
            - TASK-013
            - TASK-014
        - id: R-003
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time exports SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) bound to the configured zone: timeZone, toFormat, parse, parseFrom,
            begin, end, beginOfNextDay, today, now, toWallClock and fromWallClock.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: services/date-time
          task_refs:
            - TASK-004
            - TASK-005
        - id: R-004
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            Display surfaces format an instant as the wall clock of the configured zone: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime and sdTimeDifferent (and through them
            sd-table cells and aggregate MIN/MAX), the read-only text of sd-date-range (query-bar chips), history viewDateTime, form-generic read-only display
            and builder inspector default display, the import-excel column transform pipe, and file-explorer modifiedAt including its today/yesterday labels.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: pipes
          task_refs:
            - TASK-006
            - TASK-007
            - TASK-008
            - TASK-009
            - TASK-010
            - TASK-011
            - TASK-012
            - TASK-013
            - TASK-014
        - id: R-005
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            sd-date, sd-date-range and sd-datetime show an instant model as the calendar day or wall clock of the configured zone, keep picker and control Date
            objects as wall-clock carriers, serialize transform (ISOString/UTCString) from that wall clock in the zone (00:00 of the day for date inputs),
            resolve TODAY, min/max bounds and the date adapter today() (calendar today marker, Now button) in the zone, and, when a zone is configured, register
            the model value instead of the carrier in a parent form even without transform.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: forms
          task_refs:
            - TASK-008
            - TASK-009
        - id: R-006
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            sd-table server filters (date, daterange, datetime and range payloads), client-side filtering (row values parsed through the service), local sort,
            aggregate parsing and Excel export compute in the configured zone: a day filter is [begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day) in the zone
            and a datetime filter reads the zone-less value as wall clock in the zone; in local mode every bound equals the current value. Row-value parsing at
            the client filter, local sort, aggregate and min/max sites also stays exactly as today in local mode.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: components/table
          task_refs:
            - TASK-010
            - TASK-011
        - id: R-007
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            With a configured zone, import-excel reads Date cells (exceljs UTC fields = Excel wall clock) and text dates parsed with the column format
            (time-only formats use today in the zone) as wall clock in the zone; the stored types stay as today (Date cell: ISO string, text cell: Date) and
            unset keeps the current values.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: components/import-excel
          task_refs:
            - TASK-012
        - id: R-008
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            form-generic resolves the today min/max boundary of datetime fields to the start/end of today in the configured zone, evaluates its rules and
            validations with FilterUtilities MatchOptions.timeZone, and formats its read-only display through a date formatter passed in its display options.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: components/form-generic
          task_refs:
            - TASK-013
        - id: R-009
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            Calendar-only values and wall-clock carriers never shift: date-only strings, zone-less wall-clock strings emitted without transform,
            sd-chip-calendar values, forms/time values and picker/control Date objects show and emit the same digits under every configuration; pipes created
            with new outside an injection context keep the local behavior. sdParseTransformedTemporal keeps its current public behavior.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: forms
          task_refs:
            - TASK-006
            - TASK-007
            - TASK-008
            - TASK-009
        - id: R-010
          type: requirement
          statement: >-
            Core UI docs (sd-core.configuration, new sd-date-time, pipes, sd-date, sd-datetime, sd-date-range, sd-table filters, sd-import-excel,
            sd-file-explorer, sd-form-generic, query-bar/query-builder backend notes) and the CHANGELOG Unreleased entry (including the form.value change for
            apps that set timeZone: sd-date Date -> 'yyyy/MM/dd', sd-datetime display string -> 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', sd-date-range { from: Date, to: Date } ->
            the range model) describe timeZone; the Showcase registers sd-date-time; every Angular line, every versions/v* lockfile and the showcase pin
            @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0; v20-v22 sources come only from npm run sync.
          source: explicit-user
          status: active
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          owner_module_id: null
          task_refs:
            - TASK-015
            - TASK-016
            - TASK-017
            - TASK-018
            - TASK-019
        - id: AC-001
          type: acceptance-criterion
          statement: >-
            Call DateUtilities.toFormat('1960-05-19T16:00:00Z', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm', { timeZone }) with 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' and with 'Asia/Bangkok' on any host
            time zone -> returns '20/05/1960 00:00' and '19/05/1960 23:00'
          behavior: >-
            Call DateUtilities.toFormat('1960-05-19T16:00:00Z', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm', { timeZone }) with 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' and with 'Asia/Bangkok' on any host
            time zone
          expected_result: returns '20/05/1960 00:00' and '19/05/1960 23:00'
          verification_kind: automated
          blocking: true
          requirement_refs:
            - R-001
          task_refs:
            - TASK-001
            - TASK-002
        - id: AC-002
          type: acceptance-criterion
          statement: >-
            Call fromZonedParts, begin, end and parse with timeZone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' around 1960 and the 1975-06-13 offset change -> fromZonedParts({ year:
            1960, month: 5, day: 20 }) is 1960-05-19T16:00:00.000Z and fromZonedParts({ year: 1960, month: 5, day: 32 }) normalizes to 1960-05-31T16:00:00.000Z;
            the repeated 23:30 of 1975-06-12 resolves to the earlier offset 1975-06-12T15:30:00.000Z; begin/end of 1975/06/12 are 1975-06-11T16:00:00.000Z and
            1975-06-12T16:59:59.999Z; parse('1960/05/20 08:00:00') is 1960-05-20T00:00:00.000Z; an instant with Z is returned unchanged; parse('abc') is null;
            toZonedParts of each result gives back the input parts
          behavior: Call fromZonedParts, begin, end and parse with timeZone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' around 1960 and the 1975-06-13 offset change
          expected_result: >-
            fromZonedParts({ year: 1960, month: 5, day: 20 }) is 1960-05-19T16:00:00.000Z and fromZonedParts({ year: 1960, month: 5, day: 32 }) normalizes to
            1960-05-31T16:00:00.000Z; the repeated 23:30 of 1975-06-12 resolves to the earlier offset 1975-06-12T15:30:00.000Z; begin/end of 1975/06/12 are
            1975-06-11T16:00:00.000Z and 1975-06-12T16:59:59.999Z; parse('1960/05/20 08:00:00') is 1960-05-20T00:00:00.000Z; an instant with Z is returned
            unchanged; parse('abc') is null; toZonedParts of each result gives back the input parts
          verification_kind: automated
          blocking: true
          requirement_refs:
            - R-001
          task_refs:
            - TASK-001
            - TASK-002
        - id: AC-003
          type: acceptance-criterion
          statement: >-
            Call isValidTimeZone with valid and invalid values -> true for 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', 'UTC' and 'Etc/GMT-7'; false for '+07:00', 'GMT+7', 'UTC+07:00',
            'Mars/Base', 'local', '' and non-strings
          behavior: Call isValidTimeZone with valid and invalid values
          expected_result: true for 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', 'UTC' and 'Etc/GMT-7'; false for '+07:00', 'GMT+7', 'UTC+07:00', 'Mars/Base', 'local', '' and non-strings
          verification_kind: automated
          blocking: true
          requirement_refs:
            - R-001
          task_refs:
            - TASK-001
            - TASK-002
        - id: AC-004
          type: acceptance-criterion
          statement: >-
            Run the 1.2.6 date and filter test suites without edits and call every changed function without options, and with two different zones in alternating
            order -> the unchanged suites pass, results without options equal 1.2.6, parse without options returns the same instant isDate validates, and each
            zoned call depends only on its own option
          behavior: >-
            Run the 1.2.6 date and filter test suites without edits and call every changed function without options, and with two different zones in alternating
            order
          expected_result: >-

        - id: A-001
          type: assumption
          statement: >-
            Every browser supported by Angular 19-22 implements Intl.DateTimeFormat timeZone with IANA history (ICU tzdata), including Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh +08 for
            1959-12-31..1975-06-13.
          source: inferred
          confidence: high
          status: confirmed
          blocking: false
          evidence_refs: []
          consequence_if_wrong: A browser with stale tzdata would convert 1960-1975 values with the wrong offset.
          validation_method: Karma (ChromeHeadless) specs assert 1960 and 1975 values; Node 22 Intl gives the same offsets.
          owner: sdcorejs-angular maintainers
          rationale: Intl timeZone support is ES2017 baseline; verified with Node 22 Intl on 2026-10-16.
          impacted_refs:
            - R-001
            - R-003
        - id: A-002
          type: assumption
          statement: exceljs returns a date cell as a JS Date whose UTC fields equal the Excel wall clock.
          source: inferred
          confidence: medium
          status: confirmed
          blocking: false
          evidence_refs: []
          consequence_if_wrong: Imported Date cells would shift by the host offset under a configured zone.
          validation_method: An import-excel spec reads a workbook with a real date cell through the excel service.
          owner: sdcorejs-angular maintainers
          rationale: exceljs converts the Excel serial with UTC arithmetic; to be confirmed by the spec.
          impacted_refs:
            - R-007
        - id: A-003
          type: assumption
          statement: '@sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 can be published from sdcorejs-ultis before the Core UI release that pins it.'
          source: inferred
          confidence: high
          status: confirmed
          blocking: false
          evidence_refs: []
          consequence_if_wrong: The Core UI release would be blocked until utils is published.
          validation_method: npm view @sdcorejs/utils@1.3.0 before tagging Core UI.
          owner: sdcorejs-angular maintainers
          rationale: Same maintainers; utils already ships through its own release workflow (1.2.6).
          impacted_refs:
            - R-001
            - R-010
        - id: A-004
          type: assumption
          statement: Existing consumers never set timeZone, so the default local behavior keeps the change non-breaking.
          source: inferred
          confidence: high
          status: confirmed
          blocking: false
          evidence_refs: []
          consequence_if_wrong: A consumer would see shifted dates without opting in.
          validation_method: AC-015 runs the existing specs unchanged with no timeZone.
          owner: sdcorejs-angular maintainers
          rationale: timeZone is a new optional field with default local.
          impacted_refs:
            - R-002
            - R-009
```
