          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Where do the zone-aware convert/parse functions live: @sdcorejs/utils adds opt-in timeZone options and zoned helpers (release 1.3.0); Core UI adds
            the configuration and a service bound to it and wires every date surface
          question: Where do the zone-aware convert/parse functions live
          selected_value: >-
            @sdcorejs/utils adds opt-in timeZone options and zoned helpers (release 1.3.0); Core UI adds the configuration and a service bound to it and wires
            every date surface
          source: explicit-user
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: public-contract
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: One date engine shared with NestJS/Next backends; no second date implementation in Core UI.
          supersedes: null
          revisit_condition: null
          convention_impact:
            candidate: false
            category: null
          downstream_refs:

            - AC-001

        - id: D-003
          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Type of the timeZone value: Open literal union SdTimeZone: 'local' | 'UTC' | 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' | 'Asia/Bangkok' | 'Asia/Jakarta' | 'Asia/Singapore'
            | 'Asia/Kuala_Lumpur' | 'Asia/Manila' | 'Asia/Shanghai' | 'Asia/Hong_Kong' | 'Asia/Taipei' | 'Asia/Tokyo' | 'Asia/Seoul' | 'Asia/Kolkata' |
            'Asia/Dubai' | 'Australia/Sydney' | 'Europe/London' | 'Europe/Paris' | 'Europe/Berlin' | 'America/New_York' | 'America/Chicago' |
            'America/Los_Angeles' | (string & {})
          question: Type of the timeZone value
          selected_value: >-

            'Australia/Sydney' | 'Europe/London' | 'Europe/Paris' | 'Europe/Berlin' | 'America/New_York' | 'America/Chicago' | 'America/Los_Angeles' | (string &
            {})

            today/now are taken in the zone; unset keeps local behavior
          question: Meaning of each value kind under a configured zone
          selected_value: >-

          source: explicit-user

          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Public shape of the Core UI service: SdDateTimeService in the new entry point @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time with timeZone, toFormat, parse,
            parseFrom, begin, end, beginOfNextDay, today, now, toWallClock and fromWallClock; method names mirror DateUtilities;
            parse/parseFrom/begin/end/beginOfNextDay/toWallClock return null and toFormat returns an empty string for invalid input; in local mode
            beginOfNextDay is exactly DateUtilities.begin(DateUtilities.addDays(value, 1))
          question: Public shape of the Core UI service
          selected_value: >-
            SdDateTimeService in the new entry point @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time with timeZone, toFormat, parse, parseFrom, begin, end, beginOfNextDay,
            today, now, toWallClock and fromWallClock; method names mirror DateUtilities; parse/parseFrom/begin/end/beginOfNextDay/toWallClock return null and
            toFormat returns an empty string for invalid input; in local mode beginOfNextDay is exactly DateUtilities.begin(DateUtilities.addDays(value, 1))
          source: approved-spec
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: public-contract
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: Same verbs as DateUtilities; beginOfNextDay keeps local mode byte-identical (the review showed end + 1 ms differs on offset-change days).
          supersedes: null

        - id: D-008
          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Public shape of the utils additions: DateUtilities: TimeZoneOptions { timeZone?: string } as the last optional argument of toFormat, parseFrom,
            begin and end; new parse(value, options?) returning Date | null; isValidTimeZone(value); toZonedParts(value, timeZone) returning { year, month
            (1-12), day, hour, minute, second, millisecond } or null; fromZonedParts(parts, timeZone) normalizing out-of-range fields like Date.UTC;
            FilterUtilities: timeZone in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions, resolveRelativeDate(relative, now, options?); a repeated wall clock resolves to the
            earlier offset and a skipped one moves forward (Temporal 'compatible'); with timeZone, zone-less and date-only strings are read from their own
            fields (never round-tripped through an instant), so their digits never change
          question: Public shape of the utils additions
          selected_value: >-
            DateUtilities: TimeZoneOptions { timeZone?: string } as the last optional argument of toFormat, parseFrom, begin and end; new parse(value, options?)
            returning Date | null; isValidTimeZone(value); toZonedParts(value, timeZone) returning { year, month (1-12), day, hour, minute, second, millisecond
            } or null; fromZonedParts(parts, timeZone) normalizing out-of-range fields like Date.UTC; FilterUtilities: timeZone in MatchOptions and
            ToEpochOptions, resolveRelativeDate(relative, now, options?); a repeated wall clock resolves to the earlier offset and a skipped one moves forward
            (Temporal 'compatible'); with timeZone, zone-less and date-only strings are read from their own fields (never round-tripped through an instant), so
            their digits never change
          source: approved-spec
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: public-contract
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-ultis
          rationale: >-
            parseDateInput is not public in 1.2.6, so a public parse is added instead of exposing a throwing internal; option objects follow AddMonthsOptions
            and MatchOptions.
          supersedes: null
          revisit_condition: null
          convention_impact:
            candidate: false
            category: null
          downstream_refs:

          task_refs:

        - id: D-009
          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Invalid timeZone handling: Reject numeric offsets ('+07:00', 'GMT+7', 'UTC+07:00') even though Intl accepts '+07:00', reject unknown ids;
            SdDateTimeService throws on first use with a message naming the value
          question: Invalid timeZone handling
          selected_value: >-
            Reject numeric offsets ('+07:00', 'GMT+7', 'UTC+07:00') even though Intl accepts '+07:00', reject unknown ids; SdDateTimeService throws on first use
            with a message naming the value
          source: approved-spec
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: public-contract
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: >-
            A fixed offset has no history and reproduces the 1960 bug; with SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION there is no bootstrap hook, so the first use is the earliest
            check.

          statement: >-
            How a form resolves its model: Each date form splits model normalization from carrier normalization: the original model value goes to
            SdDateTimeService (toFormat/parse/toWallClock); carriers keep local helpers. sdParseTransformedTemporal is a fallback only for what utils rejects
            (RFC-1123, ISO with a space separator plus Z/offset, more than 9 fraction digits, surrounding whitespace) ; the public function keeps its current
            behavior and the forms use an internal instant-only variant (null for zone-less matches) only when a zone is configured. A model Date is an instant
          question: How a form resolves its model
          selected_value: >-
            Each date form splits model normalization from carrier normalization: the original model value goes to SdDateTimeService
            (toFormat/parse/toWallClock); carriers keep local helpers. sdParseTransformedTemporal is a fallback only for what utils rejects (RFC-1123, ISO with
            a space separator plus Z/offset, more than 9 fraction digits, surrounding whitespace) ; the public function keeps its current behavior and the forms
            use an internal instant-only variant (null for zone-less matches) only when a zone is configured. A model Date is an instant
          source: approved-architecture
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: repository
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: >-
            The review found normalizeDateModel, #coerceEndpoint and datetimeModelToControl each serving both models and carriers; one function cannot be
            zone-aware for one and local for the other.

            from today(); sd-datetime gets a Core UI adapter subclass in forms/datetime that replaces the class provider and both the SdDateAdapter and
            DateAdapter aliases and returns service.now() from today(), because the Now button uses today() as date and time

        - id: D-026
          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Import path of the service inside the library: forms/** and pipes/** (and every entry reachable from services/confirm) import
            @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly, never the @sdcorejs/angular/services barrel
          question: Import path of the service inside the library
          selected_value: >-
            forms/** and pipes/** (and every entry reachable from services/confirm) import @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly, never the
            @sdcorejs/angular/services barrel
          source: approved-architecture
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: repository
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: >-
            The barrel re-exports services/confirm, which imports forms/date and forms/datetime; importing the barrel from forms would create an ng-packagr
            cycle.
          supersedes: null
          revisit_condition: null
          convention_impact:
            candidate: false
            category: null
          downstream_refs:
            - R-003
            - AC-017
            - INV-008
          task_refs:
            - TASK-005
            - TASK-007
            - TASK-009
        - id: D-027
          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Form-generic wiring: sdDisplayValue stays pure and receives a date formatter through SdFormGenericDisplayOptions (like numberFormat); rule and
            validation evaluation pass { timeZone } to FilterUtilities only when a zone is configured
          question: Form-generic wiring
          selected_value: >-
            sdDisplayValue stays pure and receives a date formatter through SdFormGenericDisplayOptions (like numberFormat); rule and validation evaluation pass
            { timeZone } to FilterUtilities only when a zone is configured
          source: approved-architecture
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: repository
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: Pure functions cannot inject; passing nothing in local mode keeps 1.2.6 results.
          supersedes: null
          revisit_condition: null
          convention_impact:
            candidate: false
            category: null
          downstream_refs:
            - R-008
            - AC-014
            - AC-022
            - INV-003
          task_refs:
            - TASK-013
        - id: D-028
          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Min/max and TODAY bounds of the date forms: #parseDateBoundary goes through the service: 'TODAY' is today() for dates and now() for datetimes; other
            values become carriers with toWallClock (datetime) or the zone calendar day (date); without a zone #parseDateBoundary keeps its current code (D-029)
          question: Min/max and TODAY bounds of the date forms
          selected_value: >-
            #parseDateBoundary goes through the service: 'TODAY' is today() for dates and now() for datetimes; other values become carriers with toWallClock
            (datetime) or the zone calendar day (date); without a zone #parseDateBoundary keeps its current code (D-029)
          source: approved-architecture
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: repository
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: The review found bounds built with new Date(value), bypassing the model boundary.
          supersedes: null
          revisit_condition: null
          convention_impact:
            candidate: false
            category: null
          downstream_refs:
            - R-005
            - AC-010
            - AC-019
            - INV-004
          task_refs:
            - TASK-008
            - TASK-009
        - id: D-029
          type: decision
          statement: >-
            Sites that parse with new Date(...) today: table-local.util (client filter), table-local-sort.util, table-aggregate.util and #parseDateBoundary of
            the three forms keep new Date(...) in local mode and use service.parse only when a zone is configured
          question: Sites that parse with new Date(...) today
          selected_value: >-
            table-local.util (client filter), table-local-sort.util, table-aggregate.util and #parseDateBoundary of the three forms keep new Date(...) in local
            mode and use service.parse only when a zone is configured
          source: approved-architecture
          status: approved
          blocking: true
          scope: repository
          owner_repository_id: sdcorejs-angular
          rationale: Native parsing and utils parsing differ for yyyy-MM-dd, numbers, RFC-1123 and Date.toString text; switching them in local mode would break INV-005.
          supersedes: null
          revisit_condition: null
          convention_impact:
            candidate: false
            category: null

            vi
          question: Ranh giới kiểm chứng nào áp dụng?

        - id: INV-001
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            Every @sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 date and filter function called without a timeZone option returns exactly what 1.2.6 returns; zoned results depend only
            on the arguments (a per-zone Intl formatter cache is allowed, no other module state).
          protected_refs:
            - R-001
            - D-001
            - D-008
            - AC-001
            - AC-002
            - AC-003
            - AC-004
            - AC-022
          task_refs:
            - TASK-001
            - TASK-002
            - TASK-003
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-001
            - EVIDENCE-002
            - EVIDENCE-003
            - EVIDENCE-004
        - id: INV-002
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            @sdcorejs/angular imports @sdcorejs/utils only through its public subpaths, and every package.json and lockfile listed in the spec pins exactly
            1.3.0; @sdcorejs/utils has no dependency on Angular or @sdcorejs/angular.
          protected_refs:
            - R-001
            - R-010
            - D-001
            - D-013
            - D-023
            - AC-017
          task_refs:
            - TASK-003
            - TASK-005
            - TASK-015
            - TASK-016
            - TASK-017
            - TASK-018
            - TASK-019
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-004
            - EVIDENCE-008
            - EVIDENCE-034
            - EVIDENCE-035
            - EVIDENCE-036
            - EVIDENCE-037

            - EVIDENCE-040
            - EVIDENCE-041
        - id: INV-003
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            Inside @sdcorejs/angular every conversion of a model value (instant, zone-less string, date-only string, consumer Date) to display text, calendar
            day, wall clock, filter bound or serialized instant goes through SdDateTimeService; outside services/date-time no library file applies
            DateUtilities.toFormat/begin/end/parseFrom/parse, new Date(<value>), Date.parse, local getters or Intl.DateTimeFormat without timeZone to a model
            value.
          protected_refs:
            - R-003
            - R-004
            - R-005
            - R-006
            - R-007
            - R-008
            - D-005
            - D-007
            - D-019
            - D-027
            - AC-007
            - AC-011
            - AC-012
            - AC-013
            - AC-014
            - AC-020
            - AC-021
          task_refs:
            - TASK-006
            - TASK-007
            - TASK-009
            - TASK-010
            - TASK-011
            - TASK-012
            - TASK-013
            - TASK-014
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-010
            - EVIDENCE-012
            - EVIDENCE-017
            - EVIDENCE-020
            - EVIDENCE-021
            - EVIDENCE-023
            - EVIDENCE-024
            - EVIDENCE-026
            - EVIDENCE-027
            - EVIDENCE-029
            - EVIDENCE-030
            - EVIDENCE-032
        - id: INV-004
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            Wall-clock carriers (picker and control Date objects, date-fns parsed control values, sd-chip-calendar cells, min/max carriers) are handled only
            with local-field arithmetic; the zone is applied exactly once per value, at the model boundary, and never to a carrier.
          protected_refs:
            - R-005
            - R-009
            - D-006
            - D-019
            - D-020
            - D-028
            - AC-008
            - AC-009
            - AC-010
            - AC-016
            - AC-019
          task_refs:
            - TASK-008
            - TASK-009
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-015
            - EVIDENCE-017
        - id: INV-005
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            With timeZone unset or local, SdDateTimeService calls DateUtilities without options (beginOfNextDay = begin(addDays(value, 1))) and every Core UI
            output, including the value registered in a parent form, equals the current release output.
          protected_refs:
            - R-002
            - R-009
            - D-005
            - D-021
            - AC-015
          task_refs:
            - TASK-004
            - TASK-005
            - TASK-006
            - TASK-007
            - TASK-008
            - TASK-009
            - TASK-010
            - TASK-011
            - TASK-012
            - TASK-013
            - TASK-014
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-005
            - EVIDENCE-006
            - EVIDENCE-007
            - EVIDENCE-011
            - EVIDENCE-013
            - EVIDENCE-016
            - EVIDENCE-018
            - EVIDENCE-022
            - EVIDENCE-025
            - EVIDENCE-028
            - EVIDENCE-031
            - EVIDENCE-033
        - id: INV-006
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            The effective zone is local or an id accepted by isValidTimeZone; numeric offsets and unknown ids make the SdDateTimeService constructor throw; the
            zone is resolved once from the root injector.
          protected_refs:
            - R-002
            - D-002
            - D-003
            - D-009
            - D-022
            - AC-005
            - AC-006
          task_refs:
            - TASK-004
            - TASK-005
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-005
            - EVIDENCE-006
        - id: INV-007
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            Calendar-day bounds are computed with calendar arithmetic in the zone (begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day), never by adding 24 hours;
            a day filter is [begin, beginOfNextDay).
          protected_refs:
            - R-001
            - R-006
            - D-008
            - D-021
            - AC-002
            - AC-011
            - AC-012
          task_refs:
            - TASK-001
            - TASK-002
            - TASK-010
            - TASK-011
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-001
            - EVIDENCE-002
            - EVIDENCE-003
            - EVIDENCE-020
            - EVIDENCE-023
        - id: INV-008
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            services/date-time depends only on @sdcorejs/angular/configurations and @sdcorejs/utils; forms/** and pipes/** import
            @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly and never the services barrel; services/date-time imports no pipes, forms, components or modules entry
            point.
          protected_refs:
            - R-003
            - D-007
            - D-026
            - AC-017
          task_refs:
            - TASK-005
            - TASK-007
            - TASK-009
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-008
            - EVIDENCE-009
            - EVIDENCE-014
            - EVIDENCE-019
        - id: INV-009
          type: invariant
          statement: >-
            With a configured zone, the control that sd-date, sd-datetime and sd-date-range register in a parent form carries the model value; without a zone it
            carries exactly today's value (sd-date: Date, sd-datetime: display string, sd-date-range: { from: Date, to: Date }).
          protected_refs:
            - R-005
            - D-014
            - D-024
            - AC-018
            - AC-015
          task_refs:
            - TASK-008
            - TASK-009
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-015
            - EVIDENCE-016
            - EVIDENCE-017
            - EVIDENCE-018
        - id: INV-010
          type: invariant
          statement: The date pipes work with and without an injection context; without one they behave exactly as the local mode.
          protected_refs:
            - R-009
            - D-016
            - D-025
            - AC-015
          task_refs:
            - TASK-006
            - TASK-007
          evidence_refs:
            - EVIDENCE-010
            - EVIDENCE-011
            - EVIDENCE-012
            - EVIDENCE-013

AC-016","type":"acceptance-criterion"},{"id":"AC-017","type":"acceptance-criterion"},{"id":"AC-018","type":"acceptance-criterion"},{"id":"AC-019","type":"acceptance-criterion"},{"id":"AC-020","type":"acceptance-criterion"},{"id":"AC-021","type":"acceptance-criterion"},{"id":"AC-022","type":"acceptance-criterion"},{"id":"A-001","type":"assumption"},{"id":"A-002","type":"assumption"},{"id":"A-003","type":"assumption"},{"id":"A-004","type":"assumption"},{"id":"D-001","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-002","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-003","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-004","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-005","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-006","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-007","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-008","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-009","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-010","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-011","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-012","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-013","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-014","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-015","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-016","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-017","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-018","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-019","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-020","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-021","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-022","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-023","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-024","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-025","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-026","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-027","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-028","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-029","type":"decision"},{"id":"D-030","type":"decision"},{"id":"INV-001","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-002","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-003","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-004","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-005","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-006","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-007","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-008","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-009","type":"invariant"},{"id":"INV-010","type":"invariant"}],"revision":5,"tombstones":[]}],"records":[{"id":"R-001","owner_module_id":"src/fns/date","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-ultis","source":"explicit-user","statement":"@sdcorejs/utils
          1.3.0 (minor): DateUtilities gains TimeZoneOptions ({ timeZone?: string }, an IANA id) as the last optional argument of toFormat, parseFrom, begin and
          end, a new public lenient parse(value, options?) returning Date | null for every input isDate accepts, plus isValidTimeZone, toZonedParts and
          fromZonedParts; FilterUtilities gains timeZone in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions so date-today, date-relative and zone-less date operands resolve in
          that zone, and resolveRelativeDate accepts the same option; calls without the option behave exactly as 1.2.6; no global or module-level state; the
          dates-time-zones guide, README and changeset describe the
          API.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-001","TASK-002","TASK-003"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-002","owner_module_id":"configurations","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"ISdCoreConfiguration
          gains timeZone?: SdTimeZone, an open literal union of 'local', common IANA ids and any other IANA id, read from the root injector only; unset or
          'local' keeps the current browser-local behavior; a numeric offset or unknown id throws a clear Error on the first use of the date/time
          service.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-003","owner_module_id":"services/date-time","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"@sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time
          exports SdDateTimeService (providedIn root) bound to the configured zone: timeZone, toFormat, parse, parseFrom, begin, end, beginOfNextDay, today,
          now, toWallClock and
          fromWallClock.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-004","owner_module_id":"pipes","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"Display
          surfaces format an instant as the wall clock of the configured zone: sdFormatDate, sdFormatDatetime and sdTimeDifferent (and through them sd-table
          cells and aggregate MIN/MAX), the read-only text of sd-date-range (query-bar chips), history viewDateTime, form-generic read-only display and builder
          inspector default display, the import-excel column transform pipe, and file-explorer modifiedAt including its today/yesterday
          labels.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-008","TASK-009","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-005","owner_module_id":"forms","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"sd-date,
          sd-date-range and sd-datetime show an instant model as the calendar day or wall clock of the configured zone, keep picker and control Date objects as
          wall-clock carriers, serialize transform (ISOString/UTCString) from that wall clock in the zone (00:00 of the day for date inputs), resolve TODAY,
          min/max bounds and the date adapter today() (calendar today marker, Now button) in the zone, and, when a zone is configured, register the model value
          instead of the carrier in a parent form even without
          transform.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-006","owner_module_id":"components/table","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"sd-table
          server filters (date, daterange, datetime and range payloads), client-side filtering (row values parsed through the service), local sort, aggregate
          parsing and Excel export compute in the configured zone: a day filter is [begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day) in the zone and a datetime
          filter reads the zone-less value as wall clock in the zone; in local mode every bound equals the current value. Row-value parsing at the client
          filter, local sort, aggregate and min/max sites also stays exactly as today in local
          mode.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-010","TASK-011"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-007","owner_module_id":"components/import-excel","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"With
          a configured zone, import-excel reads Date cells (exceljs UTC fields = Excel wall clock) and text dates parsed with the column format (time-only
          formats use today in the zone) as wall clock in the zone; the stored types stay as today (Date cell: ISO string, text cell: Date) and unset keeps the
          current
          values.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-012"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-008","owner_module_id":"components/form-generic","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"form-generic
          resolves the today min/max boundary of datetime fields to the start/end of today in the configured zone, evaluates its rules and validations with
          FilterUtilities MatchOptions.timeZone, and formats its read-only display through a date formatter passed in its display
          options.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-013"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-009","owner_module_id":"forms","owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"Calendar-only
          values and wall-clock carriers never shift: date-only strings, zone-less wall-clock strings emitted without transform, sd-chip-calendar values,
          forms/time values and picker/control Date objects show and emit the same digits under every configuration; pipes created with new outside an injection
          context keep the local behavior. sdParseTransformedTemporal keeps its current public
          behavior.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"requirement"},{"id":"R-010","owner_module_id":null,"owner_repository_id":"sdcorejs-angular","source":"explicit-user","statement":"Core
          UI docs (sd-core.configuration, new sd-date-time, pipes, sd-date, sd-datetime, sd-date-range, sd-table filters, sd-import-excel, sd-file-explorer,
          sd-form-generic, query-bar/query-builder backend notes) and the CHANGELOG Unreleased entry (including the form.value change for apps that set
          timeZone: sd-date Date -> 'yyyy/MM/dd', sd-datetime display string -> 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', sd-date-range { from: Date, to: Date } -> the range
          model) describe timeZone; the Showcase registers sd-date-time; every Angular line, every versions/v* lockfile and the showcase pin @sdcorejs/utils
          1.3.0; v20-v22 sources come only from npm run
          sync.","status":"active","task_refs":["TASK-015","TASK-016","TASK-017","TASK-018","TASK-019"],"type":"requirement"},{"behavior":"Call
          DateUtilities.toFormat('1960-05-19T16:00:00Z', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm', { timeZone }) with 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' and with 'Asia/Bangkok' on any host time
          zone","blocking":true,"expected_result":"returns '20/05/1960 00:00' and '19/05/1960
          23:00'","id":"AC-001","requirement_refs":["R-001"],"statement":"Call DateUtilities.toFormat('1960-05-19T16:00:00Z', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm', { timeZone })
          with 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' and with 'Asia/Bangkok' on any host time zone -> returns '20/05/1960 00:00' and '19/05/1960
          23:00'","task_refs":["TASK-001","TASK-002"],"type":"acceptance-criterion","verification_kind":"automated"},{"behavior":"Call fromZonedParts, begin,
          end and parse with timeZone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' around 1960 and the 1975-06-13 offset change","blocking":true,"expected_result":"fromZonedParts({ year:

          fromZonedParts(parts, timeZone) normalizing out-of-range fields like Date.UTC; FilterUtilities: timeZone in MatchOptions and ToEpochOptions,
          resolveRelativeDate(relative, now, options?); a repeated wall clock resolves to the earlier offset and a skipped one moves forward (Temporal
          'compatible'); with timeZone, zone-less and date-only strings are read from their own fields (never round-tripped through an instant), so their digits
          never change","source":"approved-spec","statement":"Public shape of the utils additions: DateUtilities: TimeZoneOptions { timeZone?: string } as the

          keeps its current code (D-029)","source":"approved-architecture","statement":"Min/max and TODAY bounds of the date forms: #parseDateBoundary goes
          through the service: 'TODAY' is today() for dates and now() for datetimes; other values become carriers with toWallClock (datetime) or the zone

          switching them in local mode would break INV-005.","revisit_condition":null,"scope":"repository","selected_value":"table-local.util (client filter),
          table-local-sort.util, table-aggregate.util and #parseDateBoundary of the three forms keep new Date(...) in local mode and use service.parse only when
          a zone is configured","source":"approved-architecture","statement":"Sites that parse with new Date(...) today: table-local.util (client filter),

          vi","status":"approved","supersedes":null,"task_refs":["TASK-019"],"type":"decision","validation_boundary":{"kind":"none","source_refs":["R-001","R-002","R-003","R-004","R-005","R-006","R-007","R-008","R-009","R-010","AC-001","AC-002","AC-003","AC-004","AC-005","AC-006","AC-007","AC-008","AC-009","AC-010","AC-011","AC-012","AC-013","AC-014","AC-015","AC-016","AC-017","AC-018","AC-019","AC-020","AC-021","AC-022","INV-001","INV-002","INV-003","INV-004","INV-005","INV-006","INV-007","INV-008","INV-009","INV-010"]}},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-001","EVIDENCE-002","EVIDENCE-003","EVIDENCE-004"],"id":"INV-001","protected_refs":["R-001","D-001","D-008","AC-001","AC-002","AC-003","AC-004","AC-022"],"statement":"Every

          state).","task_refs":["TASK-001","TASK-002","TASK-003"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-004","EVIDENCE-008","EVIDENCE-034","EVIDENCE-035","EVIDENCE-036","EVIDENCE-037","EVIDENCE-038","EVIDENCE-039","EVIDENCE-040","EVIDENCE-041"],"id":"INV-002","protected_refs":["R-001","R-010","D-001","D-013","D-023","AC-017"],"statement":"@sdcorejs/angular
          imports @sdcorejs/utils only through its public subpaths, and every package.json and lockfile listed in the spec pins exactly 1.3.0; @sdcorejs/utils
          has no dependency on Angular or
          @sdcorejs/angular.","task_refs":["TASK-003","TASK-005","TASK-015","TASK-016","TASK-017","TASK-018","TASK-019"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-010","EVIDENCE-012","EVIDENCE-017","EVIDENCE-020","EVIDENCE-021","EVIDENCE-023","EVIDENCE-024","EVIDENCE-026","EVIDENCE-027","EVIDENCE-029","EVIDENCE-030","EVIDENCE-032"],"id":"INV-003","protected_refs":["R-003","R-004","R-005","R-006","R-007","R-008","D-005","D-007","D-019","D-027","AC-007","AC-011","AC-012","AC-013","AC-014","AC-020","AC-021"],"statement":"Inside
          @sdcorejs/angular every conversion of a model value (instant, zone-less string, date-only string, consumer Date) to display text, calendar day, wall
          clock, filter bound or serialized instant goes through SdDateTimeService; outside services/date-time no library file applies
          DateUtilities.toFormat/begin/end/parseFrom/parse, new Date(<value>), Date.parse, local getters or Intl.DateTimeFormat without timeZone to a model
          value.","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-009","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-015","EVIDENCE-017"],"id":"INV-004","protected_refs":["R-005","R-009","D-006","D-019","D-020","D-028","AC-008","AC-009","AC-010","AC-016","AC-019"],"statement":"Wall-clock
          carriers (picker and control Date objects, date-fns parsed control values, sd-chip-calendar cells, min/max carriers) are handled only with local-field
          arithmetic; the zone is applied exactly once per value, at the model boundary, and never to a
          carrier.","task_refs":["TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-005","EVIDENCE-006","EVIDENCE-007","EVIDENCE-011","EVIDENCE-013","EVIDENCE-016","EVIDENCE-018","EVIDENCE-022","EVIDENCE-025","EVIDENCE-028","EVIDENCE-031","EVIDENCE-033"],"id":"INV-005","protected_refs":["R-002","R-009","D-005","D-021","AC-015"],"statement":"With
          timeZone unset or local, SdDateTimeService calls DateUtilities without options (beginOfNextDay = begin(addDays(value, 1))) and every Core UI output,
          including the value registered in a parent form, equals the current release
          output.","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005","TASK-006","TASK-007","TASK-008","TASK-009","TASK-010","TASK-011","TASK-012","TASK-013","TASK-014"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-005","EVIDENCE-006"],"id":"INV-006","protected_refs":["R-002","D-002","D-003","D-009","D-022","AC-005","AC-006"],"statement":"The
          effective zone is local or an id accepted by isValidTimeZone; numeric offsets and unknown ids make the SdDateTimeService constructor throw; the zone
          is resolved once from the root
          injector.","task_refs":["TASK-004","TASK-005"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-001","EVIDENCE-002","EVIDENCE-003","EVIDENCE-020","EVIDENCE-023"],"id":"INV-007","protected_refs":["R-001","R-006","D-008","D-021","AC-002","AC-011","AC-012"],"statement":"Calendar-day
          bounds are computed with calendar arithmetic in the zone (begin of the day, begin of the next calendar day), never by adding 24 hours; a day filter is
          [begin,
          beginOfNextDay).","task_refs":["TASK-001","TASK-002","TASK-010","TASK-011"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-008","EVIDENCE-009","EVIDENCE-014","EVIDENCE-019"],"id":"INV-008","protected_refs":["R-003","D-007","D-026","AC-017"],"statement":"services/date-time
          depends only on @sdcorejs/angular/configurations and @sdcorejs/utils; forms/** and pipes/** import @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time directly and
          never the services barrel; services/date-time imports no pipes, forms, components or modules entry
          point.","task_refs":["TASK-005","TASK-007","TASK-009"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-015","EVIDENCE-016","EVIDENCE-017","EVIDENCE-018"],"id":"INV-009","protected_refs":["R-005","D-014","D-024","AC-018","AC-015"],"statement":"With
          a configured zone, the control that sd-date, sd-datetime and sd-date-range register in a parent form carries the model value; without a zone it
          carries exactly today's value (sd-date: Date, sd-datetime: display string, sd-date-range: { from: Date, to: Date
          }).","task_refs":["TASK-008","TASK-009"],"type":"invariant"},{"evidence_refs":["EVIDENCE-010","EVIDENCE-011","EVIDENCE-012","EVIDENCE-013"],"id":"INV-010","protected_refs":["R-009","D-016","D-025","AC-015"],"statement":"The
          date pipes work with and without an injection context; without one they behave exactly as the local
          mode.","task_refs":["TASK-006","TASK-007"],"type":"invariant"}],"revision":5,"schema_version":1}
    goals:
      - id: G-001
        statement: '@sdcorejs/utils 1.3.0 có API múi giờ opt-in (DateUtilities + FilterUtilities), không đổi hành vi khi không truyền option.'
        task_refs:
          - TASK-001
          - TASK-002
          - TASK-003
      - id: G-002

        task_refs:
          - TASK-004
          - TASK-005

        statement: Mọi chỗ hiển thị, nhập, lọc, import của Core UI cho cùng kết quả trên mọi máy khi có zone; chế độ local giữ y nguyên.

    state_owners:
      - symbol: sdcorejs-angular
        state: System time zone (root SD_CORE_CONFIGURATION.timeZone), resolved once by the root SdDateTimeService
      - symbol: sdcorejs-ultis
        state: IANA tzdata (browser Intl / ICU)
    service_boundaries:
      - symbol: SdDateTimeService
        scope: app
      - symbol: table helpers (SdConvertToPagingReq, filterLocalItems, compareLocalValues, aggregate)
        scope: pure_function
      - symbol: form-generic rules (sdEvaluateFilter, sdDisplayValue)
        scope: pure_function
      - symbol: SdStrictDateFnsAdapter / datetime time-zone adapter
        scope: component
    data_flow:
      - model value → SdDateTimeService.toFormat/parse/toWallClock (zone) → carrier Date → Material picker
      - picker carrier → SdDateTimeService.fromWallClock → sdSerializeTemporalValue(transform) | model string → model-facing control → parent form
      - table filter value → service.begin / beginOfNextDay / parse → PagingReq ISO bounds
      - form-generic scope → sdEvaluateFilter(..., { timeZone }) → FilterUtilities.evaluate
    declarations_and_registration:
      - symbol: SdDateTimeService
        mechanism: '@Injectable({ providedIn: "root" }), entry point @sdcorejs/angular/services/date-time'
      - symbol: datetime time-zone adapter
        mechanism: component providers of sd-datetime replace SdNativeDateAdapter + SdDateAdapter + DateAdapter aliases
    public_exports:
      - SdTimeZone (configurations)
      - ISdCoreConfiguration.timeZone
      - SdDateTimeService (services/date-time, services barrel)
      - ɵ internal instant parser in forms/models
    remaining_contract: >-
      Không thêm component mới. Một service root làm cổng duy nhất; helper thuần nhận service qua tham số; ba form tách chuẩn hoá model/vật mang. Test:
      *.time-zone.spec.ts mới theo TDD cho từng khu vực; spec cũ giữ nguyên làm bằng chứng không đổi hành vi local.
  agent_architecture:
    required: false
    conformance_invariant_refs: []
    not_applicable_reason: Không phải track ai-agent; thư viện UI và tiện ích ngày giờ.
    contract: null
